# Skills · S · part 28

853 entries, seductive-os to segment-wizard. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **seductive-os**: Build personal magnetism as a real capability, spanning presence and embodiment, conversational craft, warmth, humour, curiosity, attraction fundamentals, social calibration, reading and respecting another person's actual interest, flirtation and consent-gated escalation, dating strategy, apps and texting, style and self-presentation, rejection resilience, approach anxiety, long-term desire, and the inner game underneath all of it (self-worth, outcome dependence, authenticity). Consent-first and script-free: it builds a more attractive person, it never teaches manipulation, pressure, deceptio… ([agentik-os/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/agentik-os/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/seductive-os) · ★1)
- **see**: Analyze an image/texture to understand its REAL geometry — true (trimmed) dimensions ignoring transparent padding, the opaque content bounds, the solid interior that's safe to render content/text over, the bright field vs a dark/burnt rim, edge feathering, shape, and whether it will distort if stretched to a target size. Returns resolution-independent fractions + pixels as JSON AND an annotated proof PNG, and self-verifies. Dispatch BEFORE placing UI content over an image, sizing/scaling a sprite or background, choosing UMG OverlaySlot/Canvas padding for a transparent texture, deciding stretc… ([oliver-io/unreal-harness](https://github.com/oliver-io/unreal-harness/tree/HEAD/plugin/unreal-skills/skills/see) · ★6)
- **see**: Watch a video (URL or local path) and answer questions about it. Use when the user shares a video URL, asks Claude to look at a video file, or asks about content in a video. Smart scene-aware frame extraction + optional OCR keep token cost low. ([jain777/claude-eyes](https://github.com/jain777/claude-eyes/tree/HEAD/skills/see) · ★4)
- **see**: S.EE (SEE) platform API integration for short URL management, text sharing, and file sharing. ([jtsang4/efficient-coding](https://github.com/jtsang4/efficient-coding/tree/HEAD/skills/see) · ★2)
- **see**: repo-brief Clone a GitHub repository to ~/ai/ and generate a structured technical brief for understanding how the project is implemented — architecture, core mechanisms, design decisions, failure modes, and a guided comprehension path. Use when the user provides a GitHub repo URL and asks for analysis, a brief, a report, or wants to understand a project's implementation principles. ([luw2007/skills](https://github.com/luw2007/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/see) · ★1)
- **see**: Capture the screen and inject a short text summary into Claude's context via an in-process VLM (llama-cpp-python). No daemon. Use to feed terminal/editor/browser state to Claude for debug, fix suggestions, or summarization with minimal token cost. ([qte77/cc-senses-plugin](https://github.com/qte77/cc-senses-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/see))

- **see-also**: Generate Wikipedia-style See Also sections — austere lists of kin objects, concepts, texts, practices, or phenomena adjacent to a given subject without arguing or explaining the kinship. The reader performs the binding. Default deployment is reflexive: produce the See Also for *this conversation* — what would appear at the bottom of the Wikipedia article our chat would constitute. Form rules: bullet list, 5-15 entries, no editorial gloss (brief disambiguating parenthetical only when truly needed), no thesis, mix of obvious and slightly-oblique entries.
